videx-wellog/docs/index.html:5:<li><code>LogController</code> - container class for grouping and controlling tracks with a common scale. It also adds support for track titles and legends.</li>
videx-wellog/docs/index.html:6:<li><code>LogViewer</code> - extension of <code>LogController</code> to add overlay and user interaction (zoom/pan behaviours).</li>
videx-wellog/docs/index.html:11:<li><code>CanvasTrack</code> - base class for tracks that renders to a 2d context (HTML5 canvas)</li>
videx-wellog/docs/index.html:12:<li><code>SvgTrack</code> - base class for tracks that work with SVG (vector graphics)</li>
videx-wellog/docs/index.html:14:<li><code>ScaleTrack</code> - basic track for displaying y-axis ticks</li>
videx-wellog/docs/index.html:15:<li><code>DualScaleTrack</code> - scale track supporting two seperate domains. For instance to show MD depth and TVD depth. Requires a <code>InterpolatedScaleHandler</code>.</li>
videx-wellog/docs/index.html:16:<li><code>GraphTrack</code> - supports rendering of multiple deta series. Different graph types are supported, with implementations of the <code>Plot</code> class:<ul>
